<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48086190' data-tags='Programming,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48086190' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/116550899908879585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hardware Attestation as Monopoly Enabler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>硬件认证成垄断推动者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48086190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48086190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 734</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48086190' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hardware attestation mechanisms verify device state to software and remote services, enabling trusted-environment features. When the attestation is controlled by a single vendor, it can lock users into that ecosystem and hinder competition. The takeaway is a push for interoperable, transparent standards to prevent security from becoming a monopoly lever.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>硬件认证机制用于向软件和远程服务验证设备状态，支持可信执行环境等功能。若认证机制被单一厂商掌控，可能将用户锁定在该生态中，削弱竞争。核心信息是推动互操作、透明的认证标准，以防安全被用于垄断工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47949027' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zed.dev/blog/zed-1-0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zed 1.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zed 1.0 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2094</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 677</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47949027' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zed 1.0 marks the first stable release of the Zed language, accompanied by its tooling and documentation. The release signals readiness for broader adoption in systems programming and showcases a focus on safe, expressive syntax and productive tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zed 1.0 标志着 Zed 语言的首个稳定版本及其工具链的发布。此举表明该语言准备迎接更广泛的应用，强调安全、可读性强的语法和高效的开发工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://zed.dev/img/post/one-point-zero/thumbnail.webp' data-full='https://zed.dev/img/post/one-point-zero/thumbnail.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48292224' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://orchidfiles.com/im-tired-of-ai-generated-answers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m Tired of Talking to AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我厌倦和 AI 交谈</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48292224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1943</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48292224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 926</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48292224' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece captures fatigue with AI-generated answers and a longing for more trustworthy, human-like interaction. It calls for clearer sources, transparent reasoning, and design choices that favor meaningful dialogue over generic polish.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章表达了对 AI 生成回答的疲惫，渴望更可信、可核验的互动。作者呼吁提供来源、提高推理透明度，并在设计上优先实现有意义的对话，而非泛泛的回答。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48085821' data-tags='Programming,AI,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48085821' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unix.foo/posts/local-ai-needs-to-be-norm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local AI needs to be the norm</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地 AI 应成为常态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1823</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48085821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 727</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48085821' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The push toward on-device, local AI is moving from novelty to necessity as privacy, latency, and data governance become real constraints for organizations. This shift demands tooling and standards for model updates, secure storage, and offline operation across devices and endpoints. Expect more investment in edge hardware, federated learning, and open models that respect user data sovereignty.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>向本地化 AI 迁移正在从新鲜事变成必要趋势，隐私、延迟和数据治理成为现实约束，促使在各设备端部署和离线运行模型。需要完善的更新、安全存储和跨设备协同的工具与标准。这将推动对边缘硬件、联邦学习以及尊重数据主权的开源模型的投入。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://unix.foo/images/brutalist_ios_main.png' data-full='https://unix.foo/images/brutalist_ios_main.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48311647' aria-label='Show summary and image'>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48311647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1737</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48311647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1359</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48311647' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic releases Claude Opus 4.8, highlighting improvements in reliability, safety controls, and developer-facing features. The update expands capabilities for complex tasks and tool usage, signaling the ongoing push to make AI assistants more usable in enterprise workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 发布 Claude Opus 4.8，强调在可靠性、安全控制和面向开发者的特性方面的提升。此次更新扩展了对复杂任务与工具使用的支持，体现了让 AI 助手在企业工作流中更易用的持续努力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48037555' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48037555' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.digitalfoundry.net/news/2026/05/valve-releases-steam-controller-cad-files-under-creative-commons-license' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Valve releases Steam Controller CAD files under Creative Commons license</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Valve 将 Steam 控制器 CAD 文件以 Creative Commons 许可发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1717</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48037555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 584</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48037555' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Valve released the Steam Controller&#x27;s CAD files under a Creative Commons license, enabling makers to study, modify, or 3D print its components. This broadens access to hardware designs and could spur community-driven mods, repairs, and derivative hardware. The takeaway is that open hardware files lower barriers to tinkering and experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Valve 将 Steam 控制器的 CAD 文件以 Creative Commons 授权发布，允许开发者研究、修改乃至3D打印相关部件。此举扩大了硬件设计的获取渠道，可能促成社区驱动的改造、维修与衍生硬件。核心意义在于开源硬件设计降低了再加工的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.digitalfoundry.net/19e3ca4a25c2c/large.jpg' data-full='https://images.digitalfoundry.net/19e3ca4a25c2c/large.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48019219' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thatprivacyguy.com/blog/chrome-silent-nano-install/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Chrome silently installs a 4 GB AI model on your device without consent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌 Chrome 未经同意在设备上静默下载 4GB AI 模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48019219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1691</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48019219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48019219' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome quietly downloads a 4GB AI model to devices without user consent, raising storage and privacy risks as silent payloads become more common. This underscores the need for transparent controls and clear opt-out options to protect user autonomy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 未经用户同意在设备上静默下载一个 4GB 的 AI 模型，增添了存储和隐私方面的潜在风险。此举凸显对静默下载的透明度、可控性以及用户撤销的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.thatprivacyguy.com/blog/assets/2026-05-04-001.png' data-full='https://www.thatprivacyguy.com/blog/assets/2026-05-04-001.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nooneshappy.com/article/appearing-productive-in-the-workplace/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Appearing productive in the workplace</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在职场中营造高效形象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1570</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48038001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 636</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48038001' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>People can project productivity through visible habits and communication signals at work. The piece argues that appearance can influence collaboration and advancement, but genuine impact remains essential. For engineers and tech teams, the takeaway is to align presentation with real value by delivering tangible results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讨论通过可见的工作习惯和沟通信号来塑造高效形象。它认为外观的专业性会影响协作与晋升，但真正的成效仍然关键。对工程师而言，核心在于让自我认知与实际产出保持一致。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48067119' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reclaimthenet.org/google-broke-recaptcha-for-de-googled-android-users' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google broke reCAPTCHA for de-googled Android users</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌对去谷歌化 Android 用户的 reCAPTCHA 失效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1491</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48067119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 552</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48067119' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report claims reCAPTCHA no longer functions on de-Googled Android devices, highlighting how privacy-focused setups can clash with anti-bot protections. The piece raises questions about platform control, user privacy, and the trade-offs of relying on third-party verification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称去谷歌化 Android 设备上的 reCAPTCHA 功能不再工作，凸显隐私保护设置与反机器人机制之间的冲突。文章提出对平台控制、用户隐私以及依赖第三方验证所带来的权衡的质疑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://reclaimthenet.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/BAPiBKSUNDTJ-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://reclaimthenet.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/BAPiBKSUNDTJ-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47989883' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47989883' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/microsoft/vscode/pull/310226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VS Code inserting &#x27;Co-Authored-by Copilot&#x27; into commits regardless of usage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VS Code 无论是否使用都在提交中插入“Co-Authored-by Copilot”标签</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47989883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1485</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47989883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 821</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47989883' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bug in the editor causes commits to automatically include a Co-Authored-by: Copilot trailer, even when Copilot wasn&#x27;t used. The behavior fragments authorship signals in Git metadata and could misattribute work, prompting a patch in the works. This highlights the broader challenge of AI-assisted attribution within developer tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>编辑器存在一个错误，会在提交中自动插入 Co-Authored-by: Copilot 标签，即使未使用 Copilot。该行为会导致 Git 元数据中的署名信息失真，促使相关修复被推进。这凸显了在开发工具中处理 AI 署名的广泛挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9be2ad8fbe999c93412bba9089aaf7e6e780212a98744a40d7294d2a016eec99/microsoft/vscode/pull/310226' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9be2ad8fbe999c93412bba9089aaf7e6e780212a98744a40d7294d2a016eec99/microsoft/vscode/pull/310226' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/model-disproves-discrete-geometry-conjecture/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An OpenAI model has disproved a central conjecture in discrete geometry</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 模型推翻离散几何中的一个中心猜想</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48212493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1398</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48212493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1022</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48212493' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An OpenAI model challenges a long-standing assumption in discrete geometry by providing evidence against a central conjecture. The result signals AI-assisted math progress and could reshape how researchers tackle stubborn problems, blending machine intuition with theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项研究表明 OpenAI 的模型提出了对离散几何中一个核心猜想的反例证据。这一进展展示了 AI 辅助的数学探索潜力，可能改变研究者攻克顽固问题的思路。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48302745' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1348</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48302745' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 757</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflection arguing for institutional acceptance of taking full days off as a normal productivity and wellbeing tool rather than a luxury or punishment. It emphasizes that deliberate rest, clear boundaries and team norms around time off improve long-term output and morale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张把整天休息视为常规的生产力与幸福工具，而非特权或惩罚。强调有意识的休息、明确的界限和团队对请假的共识，能提升长期产出与士气。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bricks and Minifigs 偷走了一名男子价值 20 万美元的乐高收藏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48314136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1310</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48314136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 590</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48314136' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bricks and Minifigs, a well-known toy shop, is alleged to have taken a man’s $200k LEGO collection, revealing tensions around provenance, possession, and accountability in collectibles. The incident underscores risks when high-value items pass through small businesses and retailers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bricks and Minifigs 这家知名玩具店被指控盗走一名男子价值 20 万美元的乐高收藏，暴露了收藏品在流通过程中的所有权、保管与责任等问题。此事凸显高价值物品在小商家手中流转时的风险与挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/world-at-work/cloudflare-cut-over-1100-jobs-2026-05-07/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare to cut about 20% of its workforce</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48054423' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48054423' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 950</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48054423' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare plans to cut about 1,100 jobs, roughly 20% of its staff, as part of a strategic restructuring amid macro pressures. The move reflects broader tech layoff trends and the tension between growth, profitability, and the resilience of critical internet infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 将裁员约 20%，涉及约 1100 名员工，作为战略重组的一部分。此举反映出科技行业的裁员潮以及在维持关键互联网基础设施的同时寻求盈利与增长的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48299753' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48299753' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.youtube/news-and-events/improving-ai-labels-viewers-creators/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>YouTube to automatically label AI-generated videos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>YouTube 将自动标注 AI 生成视频</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48299753' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48299753' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 801</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48299753' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>YouTube is rolling out automated labeling for AI-generated videos to help viewers identify synthetic content. The system aims to increase transparency and reduce misinformation, with tags appearing in search, recommendations, and thumbnails. The move will affect creators who leverage AI and could gradually normalize disclosure signals across the platform.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>YouTube 正在推出自动标注 AI 生成视频的功能，以帮助观众辨识合成内容。该机制旨在提升透明度并降低误导风险，标签可能出现在搜索与推荐等位面。此举将影响使用 AI 的创作者，并推动在平台上逐步规范披露信号。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://variety.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/10/YouTube.png?crop=0px%2C8px%2C1024px%2C576px&amp;resize=1000%2C563' data-full='https://variety.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/10/YouTube.png?crop=0px%2C8px%2C1024px%2C576px&amp;resize=1000%2C563' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48324712' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.owenmcgrann.com/p/the-dead-economy-theory' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The dead economy theory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>死亡经济理论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48324712' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1291</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48324712' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1393</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48324712' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative essay arguing that long-standing structural limits may suppress broad economic prosperity despite AI and automation hype. The piece challenges easy narratives of unstoppable growth and invites readers to scrutinize productivity, distribution, and policy levers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇挑衅性的文章声称，即使在AI与自动化成为热词的背景下，长期存在的结构性约束也可能压抑广泛的经济繁荣。文章质疑单纯的增长乐观，并促使读者审视生产力、分配与政策杠杆。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48220647' data-tags='Hardware,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48220647' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.flipper.net/flipper-one-we-need-your-help/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flipper One – we need your help</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flipper One——需要你的帮助</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48220647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48220647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 472</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flipper One team is calling on the community for support to finish the device, soliciting testers, feedback, and funding. The post outlines the roadmap and invites collaboration from hobbyists and developers, emphasizing a community-driven approach to hardware hacking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Flipper One 团队向社区求助，寻求测试、反馈和资金支持以推进设备开发。文章概述了路线图，欢迎爱好者和开发者共同参与，强调以社区驱动的硬件开发模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.flipper.net/content/images/size/w1200/2026/05/Flipper-One-we-need-your-help-main-v2.jpg' data-full='https://blog.flipper.net/content/images/size/w1200/2026/05/Flipper-One-we-need-your-help-main-v2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48272984' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48272984' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nolanlawson.com/2026/05/25/using-ai-to-write-better-code-more-slowly/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using AI to write better code more slowly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 AI 慢下来写出更好的代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48272984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48272984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 443</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48272984' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI can help you write code, but slowing down can improve quality. The piece argues against rushing to rely on AI; instead implement deliberate review, stronger tests, and iterative refactoring. The key takeaway: use AI as a co-pilot, not a speed boost.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 可以协助写代码，但放慢节奏能提升质量。文章反对靠 AI 提速的做法，主张进行有意识的评审、加强测试和迭代重构。核心在于将 AI 视为合作者，而非提速工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nolanlawson.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/favicon.png?w=32' data-full='https://nolanlawson.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/favicon.png?w=32' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48109224' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Health,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48109224' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/bambu-lab-abusing-open-source-social-contract/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bambu Lab is abusing the open source social contract</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bambu Lab 滥用开源社会契约</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48109224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48109224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 378</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48109224' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A strong critique argues Bambu Lab is deviating from the open-source social contract by locking down firmware or restricting community access to designs. The post frames this as part of a broader tension between consumer hardware startups and OSS norms, with potential implications for trust and ecosystem health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇强烈的批评认为 Bambu Lab 偏离了开源社会契约，通过锁定固件或限制社区对设计的获取来压缩开源承诺。文中将此视为消费硬件初创公司与开源规范之间紧张关系的一部分，可能影响信任与生态系统健康。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48225297' data-tags='Space,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48225297' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://valhovey.github.io/gaia-mary/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Project Hail Mary – Stellar Navigation Chart</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>海尔麦里计划——恒星导航图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48225297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48225297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 235</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48225297' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Project Hail Mary presents a Stellar Navigation Chart—an interactive visualization mapping star positions and routes for navigation and astrophysical exploration. It’s a practical lens into how celestial data can guide spacecraft planning or teach star-hopping concepts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>海尔麦里计划的恒星导航图提供了一个可交互的星图可视化，便于研究者和学生直观地查看星位和导航路径。 它将天文学与太空航行的实际应用连接起来，帮助理解星际导航的原理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://valhovey.github.io/gaia-mary/featured.png' data-full='https://valhovey.github.io/gaia-mary/featured.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48182754' data-tags='Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48182754' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/18/elon-musk-has-lost-his-lawsuit-against-sam-altman-and-openai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Elon Musk has lost his lawsuit against Sam Altman and OpenAI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马斯克对 OpenAI 诉讼败诉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48182754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1081</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48182754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 578</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48182754' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A US court ruled against Elon Musk in his lawsuit against Sam Altman and OpenAI. The decision halts his bid to challenge OpenAI’s conduct in court and underscores the uphill road for high-profile disputes in the AI leadership circle.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国法院裁定马斯克在针对萨姆·奥特曼与 OpenAI 的诉讼中败诉。裁决终结了他提出的对 OpenAI 行为的诉讼，凸显了 AI 领域高知名度对立中的法律挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/elon-musk-world-economic-forum1.jpg?resize=1200,800' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/elon-musk-world-economic-forum1.jpg?resize=1200,800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48296794' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48296794' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/May/27/product-market-fit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I think Anthropic and OpenAI have found product-market fit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我认为 Anthropic 与 OpenAI 已找到产品-市场契合点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48296794' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1080</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48296794' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48296794' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues that Anthropic and OpenAI have achieved product-market fit, suggesting their APIs and tooling are resonating with developers and enterprises. The view reflects a maturing AI ecosystem where practical, scalable offerings begin to outpace novelty. It highlights what successful AI tooling needs to scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为 Anthropic 与 OpenAI 已找到产品-市场契合点，表明它们的 API 与工具已经得到开发者和企业的共鸣。这一观点反映出 AI 生态系统逐步成熟，实用、可扩展的产品开始超越新奇性。也点出成功的 AI 工具要能实现规模化的关键要素。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48100706' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48100706' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tanstack.com/blog/npm-supply-chain-compromise-postmortem' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Postmortem: TanStack NPM supply-chain compromise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TanStack npm 供应链安全事后分析</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48100706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1069</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48100706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 449</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48100706' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A postmortem documents a TanStack npm supply-chain compromise, showing how a malicious change entered a published package and propagated to downstream projects. It outlines root causes, remediation efforts, and practical lessons for the npm ecosystem—emphasizing stronger access controls, monitoring, and package signing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>事后分析揭示 TanStack 的 npm 供应链被入侵，显示恶意变更如何进入已发布包并波及下游项目。文中给出根因、纠正措施，以及对 npm 生态的实际教训，强调加强访问控制、监控与包签名的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tanstack.com/assets/og-C0HGjoLl.png' data-full='https://tanstack.com/assets/og-C0HGjoLl.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48241890' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48241890' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/05/22/us/politics/green-card-changes-trump.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Green card seekers must leave U.S. to apply, Trump administration says</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>申请绿卡者必须离开美国办理，政府称</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48241890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1064</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48241890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1879</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48241890' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The policy change requires green-card seekers to file from outside the U.S., a shift that complicates immigration pathways for international workers and students in tech. The move has potential ripple effects on hiring, mobility, and global engineering teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政策规定申请绿卡者必须在美国境外提交申请，此举可能使国际科技工作者的移民路径更为复杂，并对跨国招聘与团队协作产生连锁影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48296649' data-tags='Privacy,Web,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48296649' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcgamer.com/hardware/duckduckgos-ai-free-search-saw-nearly-28-percent-more-visits-in-the-week-following-googles-insistence-that-people-love-ai-mode/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DuckDuckGo search saw 28% more visits after Google said people love AI mode</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DuckDuckGo 搜索在 Google 宣称人们喜爱 AI 模式后访量上涨 28%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48296649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1062</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48296649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 514</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48296649' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DuckDuckGo saw roughly 28% more visits in the week after Google publicly touted AI mode, signaling interest in AI-enabled search options beyond the big players. The shift hints at how privacy-focused users respond to mainstream AI features and where alternative search engines fit in.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Google 公布 AI 模式受欢迎的一周内，DuckDuckGo 的访问量增加了约 28%，显示 AI 功能对隐私导向用户的吸引力。此现象揭示隐私取向的用户在主流 AI 功能出现时如何寻找替代方案，以及替代搜索引擎的定位。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/CiEBkxLPHniUeZDcJCDbD7-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/CiEBkxLPHniUeZDcJCDbD7-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48269961' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48269961' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/software/linux/california-moves-to-exempt-linux-from-its-upcoming-age-verification-law-after-backlash-over-forcing-operating-systems-to-collect-users-ages-amendment-proposed-by-the-same-lawmaker-who-wrote-the-original-law' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>California moves to exempt Linux from its age-verification law after backlash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州拟豁免 Linux 免受年龄验证法约束</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48269961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1054</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48269961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 487</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48269961' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California has moved to exempt Linux from its upcoming age-verification requirements after backlash. The decision highlights tensions between consumer protection rules and the realities of open-source software distribution, where collecting ages is impractical.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州在强烈反对声中决定豁免 Linux 不受即将实施的年龄验证规定约束。此举凸显了消费保护法规与开源软件分发现实之间的矛盾，以及对 Linux 这类开源系统难以强制收集年龄信息的现实。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/Nhs4yvdLbHSMdgjnZmv4wn-1024-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/Nhs4yvdLbHSMdgjnZmv4wn-1024-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48279316' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48279316' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/spain-blocks-prediction-markets-polymarket-kalshi-over-lack-gambling-licences-2026-05-26/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spain blocks prediction markets Polymarket, Kalshi over lack of gambling licence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙因缺乏赌博许可而封锁 Polymarket 与 Kalshi 预测市场</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48279316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1053</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48279316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 483</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48279316' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spain blocks Polymarket and Kalshi prediction markets for lacking gambling licenses, signaling tighter European regulation for consumer-facing betting-style platforms. The move could constrain innovative fintech startups operating in Spain and set a precedent for licensing requirements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙因缺乏赌博许可，阻止 Polymarket 与 Kalshi 等预测市场运营，显示欧洲对面向消费者的预测交易平台的监管趋严。此举可能限制在西班牙运营的创新金融科技公司，并为许可制度设立新基准。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48206768' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48206768' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.alqst.org/ar/posts/1190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta blocks human rights accounts from reaching audiences in Saudi Arabia, UAE</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 限制人权相关账号在沙特阿拉伯、阿联酋的传播</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48206768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1041</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48206768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 446</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48206768' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta&#x27;s policy move limits human rights accounts&#x27; reach in Saudi Arabia and the UAE, reflecting the complex interplay between platform moderation, local laws, and geopolitical sensitivities. The decision raises questions about access to information and advocacy in restrictive markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 限制人权相关账号在沙特和阿联酋的传播范围，体现了平台治理、当地法规与地缘政治之间的错综关系。此举引发了对在限制性市场获取信息与倡导的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.alqst.org/uploads/photos/shares/news/2026-05/6a0d7f2d38e34.jpeg' data-full='https://www.alqst.org/uploads/photos/shares/news/2026-05/6a0d7f2d38e34.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48062117' data-tags='Business,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48062117' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/poland-economy-growth-g20-gdp-26fe06e120398410f8d773ba5661e7aa' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Poland is now among the 20 largest economies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>波兰现已跻身全球前二十大经济体：原因何在</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48062117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1034</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48062117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 837</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48062117' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Poland has entered the global top 20 economies by GDP, driven by resilient domestic demand, investment, and productivity gains from reforms and EU funding. This shift reshapes European dynamics and could influence policy in energy, labor, and technology sectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>波兰凭借国内需求韧性、投资增长以及改革与欧盟资金带来的生产力提升，GDP规模跃入全球前二十。此举重新塑造了欧洲格局，未来在能源、劳动力与科技领域的政策走向值得关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/909dda7/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4923x3280+3+0/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2Fe4%2Fdf%2F057b32831d24160d51408147c477%2F88cbd023c7c44085abb11287ab911ccf' data-full='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/909dda7/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4923x3280+3+0/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2Fe4%2Fdf%2F057b32831d24160d51408147c477%2F88cbd023c7c44085abb11287ab911ccf' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48207660' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48207660' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/github-confirms-breach-of-3-800-repos-via-malicious-vscode-extension/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub confirms breach of 3,800 repos via malicious VSCode extension</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 技术泄露：通过恶意 VSCode 扩展窃取约 3,800 个仓库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48207660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1020</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48207660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 449</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48207660' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub confirms a breach where a malicious VSCode extension accessed about 3,800 repositories. The incident underscores the risk of developer tooling being weaponized in the software supply chain; it prompts re-review of extension integrity and credential hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>恶意 VSCode 扩展导致约 3,800 个仓库受影响。该事件凸显开发工具被滥用的供应链风险，敦促对扩展安全性和凭证管理进行加强审查。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/05/20/GitHub.jpg' data-full='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/05/20/GitHub.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48341578' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://consumerrights.wiki/w/Microsoft_Office_2019_and_2021_for_Mac_view-only_conversion_(2026)' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft Office 2019 and 2021 for Mac view-only conversion</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48341578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 999</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48341578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 369</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48341578' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a view-only conversion feature for Office 2019 and 2021 on Mac, affecting how documents are shared and edited. The change has implications for collaboration, licensing, and cross-platform workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了 Mac 版 Office 2019 与 2021 的只读转换功能，影响文档分享与编辑权限。此变更对协作流程、授权与跨平台工作流有实际影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48090029' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.k10s.dev/im-going-back-to-writing-code-by-hand/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m going back to writing code by hand</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我将回归手写代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48090029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 965</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48090029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 589</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48090029' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues for returning to hand-writing code to sharpen thinking, reduce tooling fatigue, and surface core problems before heavy IDE focus. The post offers practices that emphasize conceptual clarity over automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张回归手写代码，以提升思维清晰度、减少对工具的依赖，并更早地暴露本质问题。文章给出强调概念清晰度勝于过度自动化的实践建议。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48196570' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48196570' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/gemini-3-5/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini 3.5 Flash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini 3.5 闪速版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48196570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 943</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48196570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 649</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48196570' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google unveils Gemini 3.5 Flash, a speed-optimized variant in the Gemini family aimed at faster inference and snappier tool use in real-world apps. The update underscores Google&#x27;s push to edge AI speed and integration, signaling that latency remains a key performance lever even as models grow larger.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌推出 Gemini 3.5 闪速版，聚焦推理速度与工具使用体验的优化。此举凸显谷歌在提升 AI 响应性和系统集成方面的持续投入，说明在大模型时代，延迟仍是重要的性能指标。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini-3-5__keywordstatement__metacard__light.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini-3-5__keywordstatement__metacard__light.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48195009' data-tags='Education,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48195009' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://virtualosmuseum.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I’ve built a virtual museum with nearly every operating system you can think of</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我用几乎所有你能想到的操作系统搭建了一个虚拟博物馆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48195009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 940</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48195009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 218</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48195009' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal project curates a virtual museum of operating systems across history, spanning from legacy to modern platforms. It combines emulation, demos, and documentation to preserve software heritage and let readers compare how OS design evolved.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个个人项目把各式各样的操作系统整理成一个虚拟博物馆。它通过仿真、演示和文档，帮助保存软件历史并让用户比较不同操作系统的设计演进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://virtualosmuseum.org/images/front_page/0.%20Launcher%20main%20window.png' data-full='https://virtualosmuseum.org/images/front_page/0.%20Launcher%20main%20window.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48207004' data-tags='Finance,Business,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48207004' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lesnumeriques.com/banque-en-ligne/adieu-visa-et-mastercard-130-millions-d-europeens-basculent-vers-un-paiement-100-souverain-des-2026-n250918.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Goodbye Visa and Mastercard: 130M Europeans switching to sovereign payment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>再见 Visa 与 Mastercard：1.3亿欧洲人转向主权支付</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48207004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 928</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48207004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 747</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48207004' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe appears set to shift away from Visa and Mastercard toward a sovereign payments framework, with an estimated 130 million Europeans expected to adopt the new rails by 2026. The move signals a push for digital sovereignty and domestic payment infrastructure to reduce dependence on US-based networks, with implications for banks, fintechs, and merchants. It could redefine the balance of power in European payments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲似乎将摆脱 Visa 与 Mastercard，转向主权支付体系，预计到2026年将有约1.3亿 Europeans 采用新支付通道。这一举措标志着对数字主权和本地支付基础设施的推进，旨在降低对美国网络的依赖，并可能改变银行、金融科技公司和商户在欧洲支付领域的格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.lesnumeriques.com/optim/news/25/250918/2079666a-adieu-visa-et-mastercard-130-millions-d-europeens-basculent-vers-un-paiement-100-souverain-des-2026__1200_678__0-192-4096-2342.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.lesnumeriques.com/optim/news/25/250918/2079666a-adieu-visa-et-mastercard-130-millions-d-europeens-basculent-vers-un-paiement-100-souverain-des-2026__1200_678__0-192-4096-2342.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48055913' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48055913' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/926458/canvas-shinyhunters-breach' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Canvas online again as ShinyHunters threatens to leak schools’ data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Canvas 在线再次上线，ShinyHunters 威胁泄露学校数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48055913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 906</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48055913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 615</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48055913' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canvas LMS came back online after a disruption tied to a ShinyHunters threat to leak school data. The incident highlights how education platforms remain high-value extortion targets, underscoring the need for rapid containment and stronger data protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Canvas 在 ShinyHunters 的勒索威胁下重新上线。此事凸显教育平台成为高价值勒索目标，强调快速遏制和加强数据保护的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/25332725/STK419_DEEPFAKE_CVIRGINIA_L.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/25332725/STK419_DEEPFAKE_CVIRGINIA_L.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48237163' data-tags='Business,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48237163' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://davidoks.blog/p/why-japanese-companies-do-so-many' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Japanese companies do so many different things</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本企业为何跨足这么多领域</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48237163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 872</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48237163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 398</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48237163' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many Japanese firms pursue a broad portfolio of businesses, often through cross-holdings and long-standing traditions. This breadth can buffer downturns but may dilute focus from core product leadership, affecting speed and profitability in tech-driven markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本企业往往在多领域布局，这与历史的企业圈子结构和风险分散思路有关。这种广度带来抵御风险的能力，但也可能削弱聚焦核心产品的能力，影响在以科技为驱动的市场中的速度与盈利。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!q5kh!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fff42a7b2-6c27-4773-a0b0-561081c55da1_1605x2000.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!q5kh!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fff42a7b2-6c27-4773-a0b0-561081c55da1_1605x2000.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48340411' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48340411' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.brethorsting.com/blog/2026/05/domain-expertise-has-always-been-the-real-moat/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Domain expertise has always been the real moat</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>领域专长一直是最真实的护城河</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48340411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 853</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48340411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 536</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48340411' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Domain expertise remains a durable moat for tech teams and incumbents. Beyond scale and data, deep domain know-how enables sharper product decisions, faster iteration, and trusted customer relationships.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>领域专长长期构成公司竞争壁垒。虽然规模和网络效应重要，但对行业洞察、客户场景的深度理解往往决定了竞争优势的持久性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.brethorsting.com/images/icons/photo-film-solid.svg' data-full='https://www.brethorsting.com/images/icons/photo-film-solid.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48100433' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48100433' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/@NMitchem/if-ai-writes-your-code-why-use-python-bf8c4ba1a055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If AI writes your code, why use Python?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果 AI 编写你的代码，为什么还要用 Python？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48100433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 844</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48100433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 895</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48100433' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take argues that if AI can write code, the rationale for choosing Python hinges less on convenience and more on ecosystem, governance, and performance considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇观点文章认为，当 AI 能代写代码时，选择 Python 的理由更多地来自生态系统、治理与性能等因素，而非单纯的开发便利。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://miro.medium.com/v2/resize:fit:1200/1*xhqxJyee2OyVxUmQF35RBw.png' data-full='https://miro.medium.com/v2/resize:fit:1200/1*xhqxJyee2OyVxUmQF35RBw.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47961319' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dpa-international.com/general-news/urn:newsml:dpa.com:20090101:260430-930-14717/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Belgium stops decommissioning nuclear power plants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>比利时暂停核电站退役计划</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 839</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 902</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47961319' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Belgium has paused its plan to decommission certain nuclear power plants, citing energy security and potential gaps in supply. The move illustrates the tension between decarbonization goals and reliable power, and could influence regional energy policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>比利时宣布暂停核电站退役计划，强调保障能源供应的现实需要。此举揭示了在实现低碳目标与确保电力稳定之间的权衡，可能影响区域能源治理与政策走向。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cp3pww9g0p5o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>David Attenborough&#x27;s 100th Birthday</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大卫·艾登堡百岁生日</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48061884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 837</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48061884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48061884' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>David Attenborough&#x27;s centennial marks a landmark career in nature documentary, blending storytelling with scientific communication. The milestone underscores the enduring role of nature programming in science literacy and global environmental awareness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大卫·艾登堡百岁生日标志着他在自然纪录片领域的里程碑式职业生涯，将叙事与科普结合，持续推动科学素养与全球环境意识。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48323683' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openpath.quest/2026/i-am-retiring-from-tech-to-live-offline/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I am retiring from tech to live offline</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我将从科技行业退休，过上离线生活</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48323683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 835</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48323683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 569</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48323683' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An introspective note about stepping back from the tech industry to embrace offline living, citing burnout, information overload, and a desire for deeper focus. It signals a broader critique of always-on culture and invites readers to consider boundaries and alternative modes of work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇关于从科技行业退出、转向离线生活的自省之作，提到职业倦怠、信息过载和对更深专注的追求。反映出对过度“始终在线”文化的批评，并鼓励读者思考工作边界与替代的生活方式。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48056227' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48056227' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xeiaso.net/blog/2026/abstain-from-install/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Maybe you shouldn&#x27;t install new software for a bit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>也许你暂时不该安装新软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 830</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48056227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 446</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48056227' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues against rushing to install new software, urging readers to pause updates while evaluating risk and security implications. Practical tips include staged deployments, prioritizing critical fixes, and verifying supply-chain integrity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张暂缓安装新软件以降低风险，敦促在评估风险与安全影响后再决策更新。文中给出阶段性部署、优先修复关键问题以及核验供应链完整性的做法。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://annas-archive.gl/blog/llms-txt.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you’re an LLM, please read this</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果你是LLM，请读取这份指南</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 829</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 439</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48234413' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative note urges LLMs to pay attention to safety and alignment, highlighting gaps between model capabilities and reliable behavior. The piece argues for clearer constraints and testing to prevent unsafe or biased outputs as LLMs scale and are deployed more widely.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇挑衅性笔记敦促LLM关注安全与对齐，强调模型能力与可靠行为之间的差距。文中主张在规模化部署中对输出设定更清晰的约束与测试，以防止不安全或有偏的结果。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://fourlightyears.blogspot.com/2026/05/i-returned-to-aws-and-was-reminded-hard.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I returned to AWS and was reminded why I left</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我回到 AWS，被提醒了为何离开</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 824</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48073201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 596</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48073201' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A first-hand account of returning to AWS exposure to cost, vendor lock-in, or outages, highlighting the tradeoffs of cloud choices. The post contrasts AWS with other cloud approaches and stresses the importance of cost management and operational simplicity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者回到 AWS 的体验再次提醒他为何离开，涉及成本、锁定和运维复杂性等问题。文章对比了 AWS 与其他云方案，强调成本控制与简化运维的重要性。</p>
