<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48356625' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48356625' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/RedHatInsights/javascript-clients/issues/492' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Malicious npm packages detected across Red Hat Cloud Services</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Red Hat 云服务中检测到恶意 npm 包</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48356625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 629</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48356625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 330</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48356625' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers detected malicious npm packages circulating inside Red Hat Cloud Services, underscoring ongoing supply‑chain risks in the npm ecosystem. The episode reinforces the importance of dependency hygiene, reproducible builds, and source provenance checks for cloud environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全团队在 Red Hat Cloud Services 内发现恶意 npm 包，暴露了 npm 生态中持续的供应链风险。该事件强调对依赖项的严格管控、可重复构建和来源可信性的重要性，尤其在云环境中。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/81b6be3beea568b6cd77bff7667f4256cb7a678931e248c9efa036a2f509345b/RedHatInsights/javascript-clients/issues/492' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/81b6be3beea568b6cd77bff7667f4256cb7a678931e248c9efa036a2f509345b/RedHatInsights/javascript-clients/issues/492' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48002136' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48002136' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/aattaran/deepclaude' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepClaude – Claude Code agent loop with DeepSeek V4 Pro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepClaude：结合 DeepSeek V4 Pro 的 Claude 代码代理循环，成本低至原来的 1/17</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 625</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48002136' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presents DeepClaude, a Claude-based code agent loop powered by DeepSeek V4 Pro, claiming a 17x reduction in cost. Highlights potential gains for automated coding tasks and iterative AI-assisted development with cheaper inference and faster cycles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍基于 Claude 的代码代理循环 DeepClaude，搭载 DeepSeek V4 Pro，成本降低约 17 倍。强调在自动编码任务和 AI 辅助开发中的实际收益，如更低的推理成本和更快的迭代速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/235b0a5a669a8949615f237b589d13b2613a93b38554e4ee4ce7e15f18e22ca0/aattaran/deepclaude' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/235b0a5a669a8949615f237b589d13b2613a93b38554e4ee4ce7e15f18e22ca0/aattaran/deepclaude' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48201316' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48201316' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/github/status/2056884788179726685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub is investigating unauthorized access to their internal repositories</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 正在调查对内部仓库的未经授权访问</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48201316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48201316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 335</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48201316' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub is investigating unauthorized access to its internal repositories, with potential exposure of sensitive code. The incident emphasizes ongoing security vigilance and the need for strong access controls and routine secret rotation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 正在调查对内部仓库的未经授权访问，可能暴露敏感代码。此事凸显持续的安全警戒需求以及强化访问控制与密钥轮换的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/2051404708766507008/ATxxTJXO_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/2051404708766507008/ATxxTJXO_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48050964' data-tags='Privacy,AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48050964' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/chrome/comments/1t5qayz/chrome_removes_claim_of_ondevice_al_not_sending/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chrome removes claim of On-device Al not sending data to Google Servers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chrome 撤回‘本地 AI 不发送数据给 Google 服务器’的声明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48050964' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome has updated its messaging around on-device AI privacy, removing the claim that on-device processing never transmits data to Google servers. The change underscores ongoing tensions between user privacy, telemetry, and model updates. For users and developers, it highlights the importance of transparent data practices in browser-hosted AI features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 更新了关于本地化 AI 隐私的表述，撤回了“本地处理不向 Google 服务器发送数据”的说法。这一变动凸显了用户隐私、遥测和模型更新之间的持续博弈，并提醒开发者和用户关注浏览器 AI 功能中的数据透明度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48012735' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48012735' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/L1v1ng0ffTh3L4N/status/2051308329880719730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft Edge stores all passwords in memory in clear text, even when unused</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软 Edge 将所有密码明文存储在内存中，即使未使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 621</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48012735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48012735' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This exposes a security risk: Edge stores passwords in memory as plaintext, even when not in use. That design choice increases the risk of credential leaks via memory dumps or malicious processes. It underscores why secure by design and minimizing in-memory secrets matters for browsers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这暴露了浏览器设计中的安全隐患：Edge 将凭证以明文存储在内存中，即使未被使用时也可能暴露。此设计增加了通过内存转储或恶意进程获取凭据的风险。再次强调从设计层面减少内存中敏感信息的重要性，以及对浏览器安全的长期关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/amplify_video_thumb/2051308082500681729/img/txKW5u6WctKrftAv.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/amplify_video_thumb/2051308082500681729/img/txKW5u6WctKrftAv.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48214449' data-tags='AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48214449' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tante.cc/2026/05/20/on-google-declaring-war-on-the-web/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Declaring War on the Web</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌向网络宣战</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48214449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 620</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48214449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 436</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48214449' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is stepping up actions that critics argue threaten the open web as it expands AI-powered products and platform leverage. The moves could reshape how publishers monetize content, how developers build on the web, and who sets Internet standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌在扩展 AI 驱动的产品与平台影响力的同时，被批评者视为在削弱开放网络。此举可能重塑出版商的营收模式、开发者的网页构建方式，以及谁在制定互联网标准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tante.cc/wp-content/uploads/2015/07/IMG_20150717_164206.jpg' data-full='https://tante.cc/wp-content/uploads/2015/07/IMG_20150717_164206.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48348578' data-tags='Programming,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48348578' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/i/status/2060746160558543217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex just found a &quot;workaround&quot; of not having sudo on my PC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codex 找到一个无需 sudo 就能在我的电脑工作的方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48348578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 619</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48348578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48348578' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reveal shows Codex-powered coding workflows finding ways to perform privileged tasks without sudo, hinting at potential privilege-escalation risks. It highlights why secure defaults and careful permission handling matter when AI-generated code is used.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>透露显示通过 Codex 的工作流，可在不使用 sudo 的情况下完成特权任务，提示潜在的特权提升风险。强调在使用 AI 代码时，需依赖安全默认设置和严格权限控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HJk9teSXMAEwKuF.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HJk9teSXMAEwKuF.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47959463' data-tags='Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47959463' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213#issuecomment-4347988313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mozilla&#x27;s opposition to Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mozilla 反对 Chrome 的 Prompt API</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47959463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 615</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47959463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 221</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47959463' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla has voiced concerns about Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API, arguing it could complicate user consent and privacy controls and fragment browser standards. The stance highlights ongoing tensions between platform features, privacy safeguards, and interoperability in browser ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 对 Chrome 的 Prompt API 表态反对，担心会削弱用户同意与隐私控制，并破坏跨浏览器标准的一致性。这一立场凸显了浏览器生态中对隐私保护、功能设计与互操作性的持续博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/051ef604bbf18878e0d34869e80cae4cb42b36e86956f6d67294e6faaf2b614a/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/051ef604bbf18878e0d34869e80cae4cb42b36e86956f6d67294e6faaf2b614a/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48220105' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48220105' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/products/ads-commerce/google-marketing-live-search-ads/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We&#x27;re testing new ad formats in Search and expanding our Direct Offers pilot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们正在测试搜索中的新广告格式，并扩大 Direct Offers 试点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48220105' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 614</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48220105' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 555</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48220105' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is testing new ad formats in Search and expanding its Direct Offers pilot to more advertisers. The initiative signals a stronger integration of promotional offers into search experiences, potentially shifting how advertisers drive conversions and how users interact with ads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌正在搜索结果中试验新的广告格式，并扩大 Direct Offers 试点覆盖范围。此举推动在搜索体验中更直接地呈现促销信息，可能改变广告的转化方式与用户互动。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/GML_Search_social.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/GML_Search_social.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48296994' data-tags='Politics,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48296994' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/may/27/canada-sweden-saab-globaleye-aircraft' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Canada to order military plane fleet from Sweden in shift from US suppliers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加拿大将从瑞典订购军机机队，转向非美供应商</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48296994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 614</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48296994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 479</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48296994' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canada plans to order a Swedish-built military aircraft fleet, diversifying away from traditional US suppliers. The move signals a strategic shift in defense procurement and alliance dynamics, with implications for industrial partnerships, maintenance, and interoperability. The contract may reshape North American defense supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加拿大计划从瑞典采购军事飞行器机队，寻求摆脱对美国供应商的依赖。这一举措体现了防务采购的战略调整及同盟关系的变化，对产业伙伴关系、维护与互操作性带来影响，可能重塑北美防务供应链。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/65d5f6f80b88938d817176d98e43c9404840ddd6/387_0_5431_4345/master/5431.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=659c9398c5ec5062376e332ab41a4aa9' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/65d5f6f80b88938d817176d98e43c9404840ddd6/387_0_5431_4345/master/5431.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=659c9398c5ec5062376e332ab41a4aa9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47988504' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47988504' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://code.videolan.org/videolan/dav2d' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dav2d</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dav2d：VideoLAN 的高性能 AV1 解码库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47988504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47988504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47988504' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dav2d is an open-source, high-performance AV1 decoder from VideoLAN designed for speed and portability. It underscores ongoing efforts to improve decoding efficiency for modern codecs across media ecosystems and embedded devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dav2d 是 VideoLAN 推出的开源高性能 AV1 解码库，强调在多平台/嵌入式设备上提升现代编解码效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://code.videolan.org/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=1.25.0' data-full='https://code.videolan.org/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=1.25.0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48092028' data-tags='Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48092028' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.privacyguides.net/t/google-account-registration-now-requires-sending-an-sms-via-phone-instead-of-receiving-an-sms/36082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gmail registration now requires scanning a QR code and sending a text message</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gmail 注册现在需扫描二维码并发送短信</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48092028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48092028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 487</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48092028' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New Gmail registration flows require scanning a QR code and sending an SMS to verify accounts. The change adds friction, raises privacy concerns, and may complic onboarding for new users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gmail 的注册流程现在要求先扫描二维码再发送短信完成验证。这一改变增加了注册难度，并引发隐私和无障碍方面的担忧，可能让新用户的上手变慢。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://forum-uploads.privacyguidesusercontent.com/original/3X/9/8/9831576af17cf56f7f57776620c4da4b4af14b7c.jpeg' data-full='https://forum-uploads.privacyguidesusercontent.com/original/3X/9/8/9831576af17cf56f7f57776620c4da4b4af14b7c.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48309233' data-tags='Education,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48309233' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.latimes.com/california/story/2026-05-27/uc-math-professors-demand-return-of-sat-for-stem-admissions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Citing &#x27;severe&#x27; math deficits, UC faculty demand a return to SAT tests for STEM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>因数学能力被认为严重不足，UC 教职员要求在 STEM 招生中恢复 SAT 考试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48309233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 608</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48309233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 805</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48309233' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UC faculty cite severe math deficits and push for reinstating SATs in STEM admissions, fueling the broader debate over standardized tests in higher education. Supporters argue tests help identify quantitative potential, while critics warn about equity and access implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>UC 教职员以数学能力严重不足为由，推动 STEM 招生重新采用 SAT 等标准化考试，成为高等教育招生标准化考试辩论的一部分。支持者认为考试能筛出具备定量潜力的候选人，反对者则担忧公平性与机会不均。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ca-times.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/6a6f7ae/2147483647/strip/true/crop/5472x2873+0+388/resize/1200x630!/quality/75/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcalifornia-times-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2F71%2F9a%2Fa7e16b5640b59762cc3e758ffc1a%2F1294188-me-uc-irvine-student-housing-001-ajs.jpg' data-full='https://ca-times.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/6a6f7ae/2147483647/strip/true/crop/5472x2873+0+388/resize/1200x630!/quality/75/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcalifornia-times-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2F71%2F9a%2Fa7e16b5640b59762cc3e758ffc1a%2F1294188-me-uc-irvine-student-housing-001-ajs.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48084432' data-tags='Legal,Culture,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48084432' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/3d-printing/louis-rossmann-tells-3d-printer-maker-bambu-lab-to-go-bleep-yourself-over-its-lawsuit-against-enthusiast-right-to-repair-advocate-offers-to-pay-the-legal-fees-for-a-threatened-orcaslicer-developer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Louis Rossmann offers to pay legal fees for a threatened OrcaSlicer developer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>路易斯·罗斯曼愿为遭威胁的 OrcaSlicer 开发者支付诉讼费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48084432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 607</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48084432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 325</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48084432' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Louis Rossmann offers to cover legal fees for a threatened OrcaSlicer developer, signaling high-profile support for open hardware and repair activism. The gesture highlights ongoing tensions between manufacturers pursuing lawsuits and the enthusiast community defending rights to repair and independent tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>路易斯·罗斯曼愿为遭遇威胁的 OrcaSlicer 开发者支付诉讼费，显示对开放硬件与修理倡议的强力支持。这一举动凸显了厂商诉讼与热衷社区捍卫修理权和独立工具之间的持续紧张关系。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/XZSzuRYxS7bRcNPtqXALUJ-1232-80.png' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/XZSzuRYxS7bRcNPtqXALUJ-1232-80.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48062178' data-tags='Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48062178' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sinceyouarrived.world/taken' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A web page that shows you everything the browser told it without asking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个网页在未征询许可的情况下透露浏览器所有信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48062178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 602</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48062178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 292</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48062178' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A web page demonstrates exactly what data a site can read from your browser without explicit permission. It highlights passive data leakage and fingerprinting risks, reinforcing the case for stricter sandboxing, fewer default disclosures, and privacy-focused browsing defaults.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个网页演示网站在未获得明确同意的情况下能够读取的浏览器信息全貌，凸显被动数据泄露和指纹识别风险。此事强调需要更严格的沙箱、降低默认披露、以及更隐私导向的浏览器设置。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sinceyouarrived.world/api/og-taken' data-full='https://sinceyouarrived.world/api/og-taken' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48072190' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48072190' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cyberinsider.com/eu-calls-vpns-a-loophole-that-needs-closing-in-age-verification-push/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU Parliamentary Research Service calls VPNs &quot;a loophole that needs closing&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国推动破解端对端加密通讯</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48072190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 599</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48072190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 411</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48072190' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France considers measures that could weaken or undermine encrypted messaging as part of a broader digital regulation push, signaling renewed regulatory scrutiny of end-to-end encryption and its privacy vs. safety implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国在打击犯罪和国家安全背景下，拟采取措施削弱或破坏端对端加密通讯，显示出对加密的再度监管压力。这引发对隐私、公共安全和创新的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cyberinsider.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/73592.jpg' data-full='https://cyberinsider.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/73592.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48272670' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48272670' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apa.org/news/press/releases/2014/04/creativity-walk' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Taking a walk may lead to more creativity than sitting, study finds (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>散步比久坐更能激发创造力（2014 年研究）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48272670' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 588</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48272670' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 232</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48272670' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Walking boosts creativity more than sitting, according to a 2014 study. It suggests teams should embrace walk meetings and brainstorming on the move to unlock higher creativity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2014 年的研究显示散步比久坐更能激发创造力。建议团队采用步行会议、边走边脑 brainstorm 的做法，以提升集体创造力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47965108' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47965108' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openwall.com/lists/oss-security/2026/04/30/10' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>For Linux kernel vulnerabilities, there is no heads-up to distributions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 内核漏洞：对发行版没有事先通知</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 588</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 533</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47965108' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linux kernel vulnerability disclosures often fail to provide a formal heads-up to distributions, delaying patch adoption. The situation highlights gaps in how advisories propagate from researchers to downstream users, affecting enterprise risk management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 内核漏洞的披露往往没有直接向发行版提供正式的事先通知，导致修补落地延迟。这暴露了研究人员、公告渠道与发行版维护之间的信息传递断层，影响企业的风险管理和修补节奏。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.openwall.com/logo.png' data-full='https://www.openwall.com/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47979583' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47979583' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://education.ti.com/en/products/calculators/graphing-calculators/ti-84-evo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ti-84 Evo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ti-84 Evo 图形计算器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47979583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 583</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47979583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 472</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47979583' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Texas Instruments unveils the TI-84 Evo, a refreshed TI-84 graphing calculator aimed at students. It aims to deliver a snappier experience and better usability to keep calculators relevant in a classroom with modern devices. The update signals continued refinement of educational hardware rather than a switch to entirely different devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德州仪器推出 TI-84 Evo，作为 TI-84 图形计算器系列的更新版本，瞄准学生市场。新机型力求提升运行体验和可用性，使在使用笔记本和平板的课堂环境中，计算器仍具相关性。此次更新体现了教育硬件的持续改进，而非转向完全依赖其他设备。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://education.ti.com/-/media/ti/education/images/global-images/logos/ti-edtech-og-social-400x.png?rev=ec2fd198-6664-4265-951e-8d68e29d91b5' data-full='https://education.ti.com/-/media/ti/education/images/global-images/logos/ti-edtech-og-social-400x.png?rev=ec2fd198-6664-4265-951e-8d68e29d91b5' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48278406' data-tags='Legal,Business,Security,DevOps,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48278406' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.politico.eu/article/netherlands-blocks-us-takeover-vital-digital-supplier/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Netherlands blocks US takeover of vital digital supplier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰阻止美企收购关键数字供应商</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48278406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 583</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48278406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48278406' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Netherlands blocks a US takeover of a vital digital supplier on national security grounds, signaling Europe’s increased scrutiny of cross-border tech deals involving critical infrastructure. Regulators may demand concessions or divestitures, shaping future M&amp;A in the digital sector.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰出于国家安全考虑阻止了一笔美方对关键数字供应商的收购，标志着欧洲加强对涉及关键基础设施的跨境科技交易的审查。监管机构或将要求让步或剥离，可能影响未来数字领域的并购动向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.politico.eu/cdn-cgi/image/width=1200,height=630,fit=crop,quality=80,onerror=redirect/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/26/phone.jpg' data-full='https://www.politico.eu/cdn-cgi/image/width=1200,height=630,fit=crop,quality=80,onerror=redirect/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/26/phone.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48051562' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48051562' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bsuh.bearblog.dev/agents-need-control-flow/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agents need control flow, not more prompts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理需要控制流，而非更多提示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48051562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 579</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48051562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 285</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48051562' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Prompt-based thinking has plateaued for coordinating multi-step AI agents. The piece argues that explicit control flow, state machines, and orchestration offer reliability and debuggability that prompts can&#x27;t. As AI agents tackle more complex workflows, control-flow design becomes essential.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用提示词来指挥多步代理的局限性逐渐显现。文章主张通过显式控制流、状态机和任务编排来提升可靠性与可调试性；当代理处理更复杂的工作流时，控制流设计显得至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bsuh.bearblog.dev/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://bsuh.bearblog.dev/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48168856' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48168856' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techspot.com/news/112410-security-researcher-microsoft-secretly-built-backdoor-bitlocker-releases.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Security researcher says Microsoft built a Bitlocker backdoor, releases exploit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>安全研究员称微软在 BitLocker 中内置后门并公开漏洞利用代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 576</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 259</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48168856' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security researcher claims Microsoft secretly built a BitLocker backdoor and has released an exploit. If true, it raises serious questions about encryption trust, potential covert access, and the need for independent audits of enterprise Windows components. The disclosure underscores why robust patch accountability and verifiable security assurances matter for widespread disk encryption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名安全研究员声称微软在 BitLocker 中秘密内置后门并公开了利用代码。若属实，将引发对磁盘加密信任、潜在隐蔽访问和对企业 Windows 组件进行独立审计的必要性等重大疑问。此事凸显对修补程序可追溯性和可验证安全性的需求。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ecopv-eu.com/en/blog-en/replaceable-smartphone-batteries-2027-eu-regulation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Removable batteries in smartphones will be mandatory in the EU starting in 2027</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟自 2027 年起强制智能手机使用可拆卸电池</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48009697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 572</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48009697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 526</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48009697' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU rules will require removable batteries in smartphones starting in 2027 to improve repairability and reduce e-waste. Regulators push for standardization and accessible spare parts, likely impacting manufacturer design choices and supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟自 2027 年起强制智能手机使用可拆卸电池，以提升可修复性并降低电子垃圾。规定推动标准化和易于采购的备用电池，将影响厂商的设计与供应链策略。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://notepad-plus-plus.org/news/npp-trademark-infringement/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trademark violation: Fake Notepad++ for Mac</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>商标侵权：伪装成 Mac 版的 Notepad++</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48006445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 568</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48006445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 256</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48006445' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Notepad++&#x27;s team warns of a counterfeit Mac app that infringes the project’s trademark, potentially confusing users and exposing them to risk. This highlights how trademark enforcement matters in software distribution and the fragility of brand trust in app stores and unofficial channels. Developers should monitor forks and counterfeit bundles that piggyback on popular open-source projects to protect their ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Notepad++ 团队警告有一款伪 Mac 应用侵犯其商标，可能混淆用户并带来安全风险。这凸显了软件分发中商标保护的重要性，尤其在应用商店之外的生态中品牌信任容易受损。开发者应警惕山寨版本对开源项目生态的侵蚀并采取监控措施。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48285592' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/@jakeorlowitz/wikipedia-is-doing-the-capitalist-thing-56a393232943' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Big tech&#x27;s anti-labor playbook has come for Wikipedia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型科技公司的反劳工手法也延伸至维基百科</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48285592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 561</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48285592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 339</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48285592' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical piece argues that the anti-labor playbook favored by big tech has infiltrated Wikipedia, shaping editor dynamics, governance, and content moderation. The piece warns that corporate pressure and labor suppression tactics threaten Wikipedia’s open, collaborative model and could impact information quality and editor rights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章批评大型科技公司用来压制劳工的手法已渗透到维基百科，影响编辑社区的治理与内容管理。警示称企业压力与压制劳动的策略可能侵蚀维基百科的开放协作模式，进而影响信息质量与编辑者权益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://miro.medium.com/v2/da:true/resize:fit:1200/0*dm2uAvSvh0Wd62kX' data-full='https://miro.medium.com/v2/da:true/resize:fit:1200/0*dm2uAvSvh0Wd62kX' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48076173' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48076173' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chrismorgan.info/no-query-strings' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I’ve banned query strings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我已禁止查询字符串</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48076173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 559</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48076173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48076173' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer bans query strings to improve cacheability and determinism in URLs. This approach reduces cache fragmentation and enables more aggressive CDN caching, though it requires rethinking how parameters and analytics are handled. The post shares practical steps and trade-offs from their experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者宣布禁用查询字符串以提升 URL 的缓存性和确定性。这一做法减少了缓存碎片，使 CDN 能更有效地缓存资源，但需要重新考虑参数和分析信息的处理方式。文中分享了基于其经验的实践要点与取舍。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48181041' data-tags='Culture,Startups,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48181041' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://radleybalko.substack.com/p/truth-power-and-honest-journalism' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Garry Tan, the CEO of YC, accused me of unethical reporting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>YC CEO Garry Tan 指控我的报道不道德</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48181041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 558</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48181041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48181041' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Radley Balko recounts Garry Tan&#x27;s accusation that his reporting was unethical, setting up a rare public clash between a startup founder and a journalist. The exchange highlights how power dynamics shape tech coverage and where accountability boundaries lie. It argues for transparent standards and robust verification in tech journalism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Radley Balko 描述了 YC CEO Garry Tan 指控其报道不道德的对峙，揭示创始人与记者之间罕见的公开冲突。此事凸显权力关系如何影响科技报道的边界与问责。强调在科技新闻中坚持透明标准与充分核实的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48006967' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48006967' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thetypicalset.com/blog/thoughts-on-coding-agents' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The bottleneck was never the code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瓶颈从来不是代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48006967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 555</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48006967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 363</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48006967' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that the real bottleneck in building effective AI agents isn’t code but the orchestration, data quality, and decision-making loops that feed them. It stresses that tooling, memory, and integration with real-world data often limit performance more than algorithms. Takeaway: invest in systems design and data pipelines, not just faster code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为构建高效 AI 代理的瓶颈并非代码本身，而是编排、数据质量与决策循环等要素。强调工具链、内存管理以及与真实数据的集成往往比算法本身更能决定成效。要点是：应更多投资于系统设计和数据管线，而非单纯追求更快的代码。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thetypicalset.com/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://thetypicalset.com/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zerostack is a Unix-inspired coding agent built in Rust, aiming to streamline command-line tooling and automation. It demonstrates how safety and performance-friendly Rust can empower developer tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zerostack 是一个用纯 Rust 编写、受 Unix 启发的编码助手，旨在提升命令行工具与自动化的效率，展示了 Rust 在开发者工具中的潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jola.dev/posts/running-local-models-on-m4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Running local models on an M4 with 24GB memory</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48089091' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical exploration of running local AI models on an M4 with 24GB RAM, detailing the trade-offs between model size, quantization, and CPU inference. It shows how modest hardware can still enable useful local inference without cloud reliance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在配备 24GB 内存的 M4 上本地运行 AI 模型的实用探索，讨论模型大小、量化与 CPU 推理之间的权衡。展示了在相对有限的硬件上实现有用的本地推理、减少对云端的依赖。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.railway.com/incident/I23M92U0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Incident Report: Railway Blocked by Google Cloud [resolved]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>铁路因 Google Cloud 阻塞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48201484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 552</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48201484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 351</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48201484' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Google Cloud outage temporarily blocks Railway&#x27;s platform, disrupting deployments and status visibility for users relying on their hosting services. This incident underscores how dependent development tools and infrastructure are on cloud providers, highlighting the need for resilience strategies like multi-cloud or regional failovers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google Cloud 的故障暂时阻塞了 Railway 平台，影响使用其托管服务的开发者的部署和状态查询。此事件凸显了开发工具和基础设施对云服务的高度依赖，强调需要通过多云或区域性故障转移等方式提升韧性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经过优化的软件仍能让十年前的 Xeon 功能不错地应对现代工作负载。该案例在 2016 年代的服务器 CPU 上展现出具有竞争力的性能，促使小型部署重新考量硬件预算。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48022742' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://idiallo.com/blog/ai-didnt-delete-your-database-you-did' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI didn&#x27;t delete your database, you did</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 没有删除你的数据库，是你删的</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48022742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 538</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48022742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48022742' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that data loss often results from human error or misconfigurations rather than AI mishaps, calling for stronger backups, robust data governance, and clearer ownership of data workflows. It emphasizes responsibility on operators and teams rather than blaming tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为数据丢失往往源自人为错误或配置失误，而非 AI 的失误，呼吁加强备份、健全的数据治理以及对数据工作流的明确责任。强调将责任落到运营团队而非单纯指责工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.idiallo.com/images/assets/652/hero.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.idiallo.com/images/assets/652/hero.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/glasswing-initial-update' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Project Glasswing: An Initial Update</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Glasswing 项目：初步更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48240419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 536</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48240419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 316</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48240419' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic provides an early progress update on Glasswing, outlining the project’s goals, scope, and planned milestones. The note signals continued investment in safety-focused evaluation and alignment research for future models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 对 Glasswing 给出早期进展更新，概述项目目标、范围与后续里程碑。该更新体现其在面向更安全的模型对齐与评估方面持续投入。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48023861' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48023861' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://susam.net/inverse-laws-of-robotics.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Three Inverse Laws of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 的三条逆向律</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48023861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48023861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 349</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48023861' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Three inverse laws of AI propose that as AI grows more capable, governance should tighten; interpretability remains essential for trust; and expectations should be tempered by practical limits. The piece uses humorous framing to underscore real-world constraints in safety and deployment. It invites readers to rethink AI governance alongside capability gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出三条 AI 的逆向法则：随着能力提升，治理需要加紧；可解释性对建立信任至关重要；对现实边界应持保留态度。以诙谐的笔法强调了现实世界在安全与落地中的约束，鼓励在能力增长的同时重新审视治理。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://audiomass.co/?multitrack=1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Audiomass – a free, open-source multitrack audio editor for the web</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Audiomass——一个免费开源的网页多轨音频编辑器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48258015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 528</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48258015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48258015' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Audiomass is a free, open-source multitrack audio editor that runs entirely in your browser. It offers a simple timeline and essential editing tools with no installation, making quick editing and sharing easy for web users. Being open-source, it invites community contributions and transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Audiomass 是一个免费、开源的浏览器端多轨音频编辑器，完全在浏览器中运行，无需安装即可完成编辑与导出。它提供简单的时间线和基本编辑工具，便于快速处理和分享音频，社区贡献也更易参与。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48182281' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/anthropic-acquires-stainless' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic acquires Stainless</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 收购 Stainless</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48182281' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 527</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48182281' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 377</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48182281' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic has acquired Stainless, a startup focused on safety tooling and governance capabilities. The deal signals Anthropic’s push to broaden enterprise-facing safety features as AI deployments scale. Integration could accelerate productization for customers and strengthen governance tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 收购 Stainless，一家专注于安全工具与治理能力的初创公司。此举昭示 Anthropic 将进一步扩展面向企业的安全功能，以应对大规模 AI 部署的合规与风险管理需求。并购有望加速对外提供的产品落地，并强化治理能力。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47962548' data-tags='Energy,Science,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47962548' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.construction-physics.com/p/how-an-oil-refinery-works' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How an oil refinery works</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>炼油厂如何运作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47962548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 527</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47962548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47962548' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An oil refinery uses distillation towers to separate crude oil into fuels and feedstocks, then refines these streams through cracking, reforming, and blending. The process is large-scale, energy-intensive, and central to energy economics, making understanding it valuable for anyone following infrastructure tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>炼油厂通过塔式蒸馏等工艺把原油分离成燃料和化工原料，再经裂解、改质和混合进行深加工。这个过程规模庞大、能耗高，是能源经济的核心，理解其流程有助于把握能源市场的运行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!AIFC!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ff98b1375-89f9-4b10-a92a-25faf8a82069_800x600.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!AIFC!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ff98b1375-89f9-4b10-a92a-25faf8a82069_800x600.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48292941' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rubbishtalk.com/economy/how-private-equity-bought-americas-essential-services/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Private equity bought America&#x27;s essential services</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>私募股权买下美国的基础公共服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48292941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 524</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48292941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 537</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48292941' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that private equity has increasingly acquired essential public-facing services, raising concerns about debt loads, cost-cutting, and long-term resilience. It highlights potential risks to affordability and access while outlining the incentives at play in capital-heavy sectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出私募股权日益收购关键公共服务，带来债务压力、成本削减和长期韧性方面的担忧，可能影响可负担性与可及性，同时揭示资本密集型行业中的激励机制。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48346947' data-tags='Health,Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48346947' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thesciverse.org/scientists-found-that-the-creatine-supplement-millions-take-for-muscle-gains-is-quietly-raising-brain-energy-levels-and-slowing-early-alzheimers-cognitive-decline-by-30/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Creatine raises brain energy levels and slows cognitive decline: study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>肌酸提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退的研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 522</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 361</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48346947' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds creatine supplementation boosts brain energy and slows cognitive decline, potentially by up to 30% in early Alzheimer&#x27;s progression. If confirmed, this could influence dietary guidelines and therapeutic strategies for neurodegenerative diseases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示补充肌酸可以提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退，早期阿尔茨海默病可能下降速度最高可达约30%。若再证实，这将影响膳食建议和神经退行性疾病的治疗策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://thesciverse.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Scientists-found-that-the-creatine-supplement-millions-take-for-muscle-gains-is-quietly-raising-brain-energy-levels-and-slowing-early-Alzheimers-cognitive-decline-by-30.webp' data-full='http://thesciverse.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Scientists-found-that-the-creatine-supplement-millions-take-for-muscle-gains-is-quietly-raising-brain-energy-levels-and-slowing-early-Alzheimers-cognitive-decline-by-30.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47961838' data-tags='Privacy,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47961838' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c5y7yvgy0w6o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta in row after workers who saw smart glasses users having sex lose jobs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 因员工看到戴智能眼镜的人发生性行为而被解雇事件引发风波</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 519</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 410</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47961838' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta is at odds after reports that employees who witnessed colleagues using smart glasses to observe sexual activity were fired. The situation highlights tensions around wearable surveillance, employee reporting, and HR policies in a highly visible tech company.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Meta 团队中一些在目睹同事通过智能眼镜观看性行为后被解雇的员工，引发了关于穿戴式监控、举报流程与人力资源政策的争议。这一事件凸显了科技巨头在员工安全与隐私之间的博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/ef60/live/2404fb20-4498-11f1-b4a4-6ff2bccf200b.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/ef60/live/2404fb20-4498-11f1-b4a4-6ff2bccf200b.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48229319' data-tags='Hardware,Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48229319' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://davidoks.blog/p/ai-is-killing-the-cheap-smartphone' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The memory shortage is causing a repricing of consumer electronics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>内存短缺正在重塑消费电子价格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48229319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 517</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48229319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 606</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48229319' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Global memory shortages are driving up the cost of consumer electronics as DRAM and NAND demand climbs for AI and data-center workloads. Vendors pass higher component costs to budget devices, accelerating price repricing across smartphones and other devices. The shift reflects broader supply-chain dynamics in semiconductors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球内存短缺推高了消费电子的成本，随着 AI 和数据中心对 DRAM 与 NAND 的需求上升，厂商将更高的元件成本传导至便宜设备，推动智能手机等产品的价格再定价。这一变化反映了半导体供应链的更广泛动态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!5Z0b!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ffb39b638-e59b-451f-af50-57065a9cbac5_800x533.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!5Z0b!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ffb39b638-e59b-451f-af50-57065a9cbac5_800x533.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国医疗保健市场将公民身份和种族等数据分享给广告科技巨头</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48011689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 516</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48011689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48011689' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report reveals that US healthcare marketplaces shared sensitive citizenship and race data with ad tech giants, raising privacy and regulatory concerns. The finding underscores the need for stricter data governance and transparency in how health data is used outside medical contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道揭示美国医疗保健市场将公民身份与种族等敏感数据分享给广告科技巨头，引发隐私与监管担忧。这凸显了在健康数据用于非医疗场景时对数据治理与透明度的严格要求。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙议会将对 LaLiga 的大规模 IP 阻断采取行动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47964034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 512</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47964034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 224</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47964034' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spain&#x27;s parliament plans to curb mass IP blockades pushed by La Liga, addressing concerns over overbroad blocks that can affect legitimate sites. The move signals a push for more measured, privacy-respecting enforcement while preserving anti-piracy aims. It highlights the tension between content protection and civil liberties in digital policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙议会计划限制 La Liga 推动的广泛 IP 阻断措施，纠正对合法站点和服务的过度封锁。此举旨在在实现反盗版目标的同时，推动更克制、尊重隐私的执法方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48047981' data-tags='Finance,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48047981' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://paulkrugman.substack.com/p/grand-theft-oil-futures' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grand Theft Oil Futures: Insider traders keep making a killing at our expense</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巨额油价期货内幕交易者继续坐收利润</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 499</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48047981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 322</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48047981' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paul Krugman argues that insiders keep profiting from oil futures, extracting wealth at the public&#x27;s expense due to market structure and regulatory gaps. The essay highlights how information advantages and opaque pricing enable rent extraction, and it calls for policy remedies to curb excess profits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>保罗·克鲁格曼指出，油价期货市场的内幕交易者持续从中获利，市场结构和监管漏洞使公众承受成本。文章揭示信息优势与定价不透明如何促成利润攫取，并呼吁通过政策改革来遏制这类过度获利。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48250144' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal exploration of writerdeck, a card-based system for organizing writing ideas and tasks. It details how the author set it up, what kinds of cards they include, and how the deck helps with ideation and planning across long projects. Useful tips for starting your own writerdeck are included.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 writerdeck 的个人探索——一种用卡片组织写作灵感与任务的系统。文章讲述作者如何搭建、包含哪些卡片，以及它如何在长期项目中帮助创意产生与计划。文末提供了启动自己写作卡组的实用要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48307887' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48307887' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lenz.io/research/llm-disagreement' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Disagreement among frontier LLMs on real-world fact-checks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>前沿大模型在现实世界事实核查中的分歧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48307887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 495</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48307887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 340</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48307887' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study shows frontier LLMs disagree on factual claims in real-world fact-check tasks, underscoring reliability limits and evaluation challenges. The findings suggest need for ensemble methods, calibration, and human-in-the-loop to prevent misinformation in critical applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示前沿大模型在现实世界的事实核查中对同一事实往往持不同结论，暴露出可靠性与评估难题。结果强调需要多模型集成、校准，以及人工在环来降低关键应用中的误导风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lenz.io/research/llm-disagreement/og.png' data-full='https://lenz.io/research/llm-disagreement/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48061566' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48061566' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://meshtastic.org/docs/introduction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An Introduction to Meshtastic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meshtastic 入门指南</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48061566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 495</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48061566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 184</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48061566' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meshtastic is an open-source mesh-networking project that enables off-grid text messaging using low-power radios like LoRa. This introduction covers core concepts, components, and practical use cases, plus trade-offs in latency and range compared with cellular networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meshtastic 是一个开源网状通信项目，利用低功耗无线电（如 LoRa）在无蜂窝网络的情况下实现短讯传输。本文概述了核心概念、组成部分和典型用例，并讨论了与蜂窝网络相比的延迟和覆盖范围权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://meshtastic.org/img/logo.svg' data-full='https://meshtastic.org/img/logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
