<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/04/microsoft-open-sources-the-earliest-dos-source-code-discovered-to-date/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft open-sources “the earliest DOS source code discovered to date”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软开源“迄今发现的最早 DOS 源代码”</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48253386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 495</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48253386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48253386' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft released the earliest DOS source code discovered to date, offering a historical glimpse into early PC software and licensing. The release underscores the trend of open-sourcing archival code for education, transparency, and research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软公开了迄今发现的最早 DOS 源代码，为早期个人电脑软件和许可制度提供了历史洞见。这一举动体现了公开档案代码以用于教育、透明度和研究的趋势。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48198551' data-tags='Energy,Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48198551' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.autonocion.com/us/tesla-lithium-refinery-texas/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla&#x27;s lithium refinery discharges 231,000 gallons of polluted wastewater a day</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉在德州的锂精炼厂每日排放约231,000加仑污染废水</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48198551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 493</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48198551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48198551' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla&#x27;s lithium refinery in Texas is discharging about 231,000 gallons of polluted wastewater daily, drawing environmental and regulatory scrutiny. The figure underscores the water-management challenges that accompany rapid EV supply-chain growth and could affect local communities and ecosystems. For engineers and policymakers, it highlights the need for robust wastewater treatment and transparent environmental reporting for new battery facilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉在德州的锂精炼厂每天排放约231,000加仑污染废水，引发环境和监管关注。这一数字凸显在迅速扩张的电动车供应链中水资源管理的挑战，可能影响周边社区与生态系统。对工程师和决策者而言，这强调需要对新建电池厂进行严格的废水处理与透明的环境披露。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.autonocion.com/us/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Tesla-Lithium-Refinery.jpg' data-full='https://www.autonocion.com/us/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Tesla-Lithium-Refinery.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48181125' data-tags='Security,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48181125' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://archestra.ai/blog/only-responsible-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We stopped AI bot spam in our GitHub repo using Git&#x27;s –author flag</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们用 Git 的 --author 标志阻止了仓库里的机器人垃圾信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48181125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 493</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48181125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 235</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48181125' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>By enforcing commit authorship with Git&#x27;s --author flag, the team significantly cut spam from automated bots in their repo. The approach blocks impersonation at the source and complements existing security controls. It shows how simple version-control metadata can curb abuse without heavy ML defenses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过在提交时强制使用 Git 的 --author 标志来标识作者，该团队显著降低了仓库中的机器人垃圾信息。此做法在源头阻断冒充行为，辅以其他安全控制。该案例证明了版本控制元数据在反滪用方面的实用性，不必依赖复杂的机器学习防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://archestra.ai/blog/2026-04-07-only-responsible-ai.webp' data-full='https://archestra.ai/blog/2026-04-07-only-responsible-ai.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48318481' data-tags='Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48318481' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260513-your-car-is-spying-on-you-its-about-to-get-worse' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cars collect a startling amount of data about you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>汽车收集的数据量令人震惊</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48318481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 490</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48318481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 284</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48318481' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modern cars collect far more data than drivers realize—from location to driving habits and preferences—raising privacy concerns as cars become more connected.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代汽车收集的数据远超驾驶者的认知范围，包括位置信息、驾驶习惯和偏好等，这在车联网日益普及的背景下引发隐私担忧。文章探讨了数据的收集、访问方以及未来可能的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nkx9w7.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nkx9w7.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48071940' data-tags='AI,Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48071940' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/trq212/status/2052809885763747935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using Claude Code: The unreasonable effectiveness of HTML</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 Claude Code：HTML 的非同寻常高效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48071940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 489</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48071940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48071940' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code demonstrates that HTML&#x27;s simple, deterministic structure can yield reliable, quick wins for AI-assisted frontend tasks. HTML&#x27;s straightforward nature makes it a practical starting point for AI-driven UI prototyping and code generation. This highlights HTML as a durable substrate for AI copilots in frontend work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 展示了 HTML 的简单、确定性结构在 AI 辅助前端任务中能带来可靠且快速的收益。HTML 的直接性使其成为 AI 驱动的 UI 原型和代码生成的实际起点。此现象凸显了 HTML 在前端工作中作为 AI 协作工具的持久价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HHz_ftzaIAAwkQs.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HHz_ftzaIAAwkQs.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47991981' data-tags='AI,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47991981' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/apr/30/ai-outperforms-doctors-in-harvard-trial-of-emergency-triage-diagnoses' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI’s o1 correctly diagnosed 67% of ER patients vs. 50-55% by triage doctors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 的 o1 在急诊诊断中正确率达 67%，超出分诊医生的 50–55%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47991981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 488</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47991981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 437</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47991981' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In a Harvard trial, OpenAI&#x27;s o1 correctly diagnosed 67% of ER patients, compared with 50-55% by triage doctors. This demonstrates AI&#x27;s potential to augment emergency triage, but real-world deployment must address safety, workflow integration, and clinician oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在哈佛的一项试验中，o1 在急诊患者诊断中的正确率达到 67%，明显高于分诊医生的 50–55%。这显示人工智能有潜力提升急诊分诊的效率与准确性，但将其落地需解决安全性、工作流整合以及医生监督等挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/7f4b1062db43fb053f4d68c08b8e92dd39f49107/0_62_4362_3487/master/4362.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=ceb8f56ddb65e715896d86461e95819f' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/7f4b1062db43fb053f4d68c08b8e92dd39f49107/0_62_4362_3487/master/4362.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=ceb8f56ddb65e715896d86461e95819f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48203698' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48203698' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.habets.se/2026/05/Everything-in-C-is-undefined-behavior.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everything in C is undefined behavior</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C 语言中的一切都是未定义行为</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48203698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 488</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48203698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 678</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48203698' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that C&#x27;s undefined behavior is pervasive, undermining safety and portability. It explains risk examples and why developers should write safer code or adopt safer languages and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博文指出，C 语言中的未定义行为无处不在，严重影响安全性和可移植性。文章通过实例说明 UB 的风险，建议开发者采用更严格的写法或转向更安全的语言与工具来降低风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48317774' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48317774' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/nasaspaceflight/status/2060164928472854821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blue Origin&#x27;s New Glenn blows up during static fire test</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Blue Origin 的 New Glenn 静态点火测试爆炸</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48317774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 486</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48317774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 534</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48317774' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Blue Origin&#x27;s New Glenn rocket exploded during a ground static-fire test, marking a major setback for the program. The accident raises questions about the design maturity of the heavy-lift vehicle and could affect schedules, budgets, and contractor confidence, underscoring the steep costs of rocket development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Blue Origin 的 New Glenn 在一次地面静态点火测试中爆炸，这是对该计划的重大挫折。此次事故引发关于大推力运载体系成熟度的讨论，可能影响时间表、预算和承包商信心，凸显火箭开发的高成本与高风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jf.x.com/images/media-preview/2060164928472854821' data-full='https://jf.x.com/images/media-preview/2060164928472854821' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://moq.dev/blog/webrtc-is-the-problem/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI’s WebRTC problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 的 WebRTC 问题与挑战</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48051951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 484</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48051951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48051951' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI’s WebRTC challenges highlight the friction between real-time media needs and browser/network realities in AI-powered web apps. The issues point to where WebRTC adoption stalls and what developers might optimize—latency, security, and compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 的 WebRTC 问题揭示 AI 驱动网页应用中实时媒体需求与浏览器/网络现实之间的摩擦。相关问题指向需要优化的领域，如延迟、安全性与兼容性，以及在实际应用中的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://moq.dev/blog/webrtc-is-the-problem/rtt.png' data-full='https://moq.dev/blog/webrtc-is-the-problem/rtt.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48024859' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48024859' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reflex.dev/blog/computer-use-is-45x-more-expensive-than-structured-apis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Computer Use is 45x more expensive than structured APIs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>计算使用成本比结构化 API 高出 45 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48024859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 483</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48024859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 263</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48024859' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Computing for unstructured tasks can be 45x more expensive than using structured APIs. This highlights the cost benefits of API-based tooling and disciplined data orchestration for AI workloads. It nudges engineers to design pipelines around reliable interfaces rather than ad-hoc compute.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项分析称，处理非结构化任务的计算成本大约比使用结构化 API 高出 45 倍。这凸显了基于 API 的工作流在 AI 负载中的成本优势，促使开发者以稳定接口而非随意计算来设计数据处理管线。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://web.reflex-assets.dev/blog/computer-use-is-45x-more-expensive-than-structured-apis.webp' data-full='https://web.reflex-assets.dev/blog/computer-use-is-45x-more-expensive-than-structured-apis.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48226588' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48226588' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prismreports.org/2026/05/20/seattle-shield-private-companies-surveillance/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Seattle Shield, an intelligence-sharing network operated by the Seattle police</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Seattle Shield：西雅图警方运营的情报共享网络</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48226588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 483</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48226588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48226588' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Seattle Shield is an intelligence-sharing network run by Seattle&#x27;s police that connects private companies and public agencies, enabling data sharing and surveillance. The piece analyzes concerns around privacy, accountability, and scope creep as public safety collaborations with private partners expand.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Seattle Shield 是由西雅图警方运营的情报共享网络，连接私企与公共机构以实现数据共享与监控。文章聚焦隐私、问责以及随着公私合作扩张所带来的权限范围扩张等问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://prismreports.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Seattle-shield-ft-image.png' data-full='https://prismreports.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Seattle-shield-ft-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48050751' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/antirez/ds4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek 4 Flash local inference engine for Metal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek 4：面向 Metal 的本地推理引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 483</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48050751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48050751' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek 4 Flash runs as a local inference engine optimized for Apple&#x27;s Metal, enabling on-device ML workloads without cloud dependencies. It aims for fast, low-footprint inference suitable for edge apps and privacy-conscious users. The project highlights ongoing efforts to bring efficient ML at the user device level.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek 4 作为面向 Apple Metal 的本地推理引擎，支持在设备上运行 ML 推理，避免云端依赖。目标是为边缘应用提供快速、低开销的推理，并强调对隐私的考虑。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48297645' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48297645' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thran.uk/writ/hdid/2025/12/simcity-3k-in-4k.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SimCity 3k in 4k (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SimCity 3000 4K 重制（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48297645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 480</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48297645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48297645' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A fan-made project recreates SimCity 3000 in 4K, updating visuals while preserving the classic city-building gameplay. The effort highlights how modern upscaling, art direction, and emulation can breathe new life into beloved 2000s games. For retro enthusiasts and streamers, it signals a growing niche of high-res nostalgia experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个玩家自制项目将 SimCity 3000 重制为 4K，保留经典玩法的同时提升画质。此举展示了现代放大、美术风格更新与仿真技术如何为经典游戏注入新活力。对于怀旧玩家和主播而言，预示着高分辨率的复古体验日益兴起。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.thran.uk/theme/img/thranman_92px.jpg' data-full='https://www.thran.uk/theme/img/thranman_92px.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48267290' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48267290' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stevemagness.substack.com/p/the-cost-of-safetyism' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What we lost when we stopped letting kids leave the front yard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当我们停止让孩子离开前院时，我们失去了什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48267290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 479</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48267290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 540</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48267290' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>What we lose when we stop letting kids leave the front yard argues that outdoor exploration builds resilience and independence, and that safety-first culture can curb curiosity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为让孩子在户外探索、承担风险有助于培养韧性与独立性，过度的安全文化可能削弱好奇心。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48026207' data-tags='Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48026207' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://variety.com/2026/digital/news/meta-ai-mark-zuckerberg-copyright-infringement-lawsuit-publishers-scott-turow-1236738383/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zuckerberg &#x27;Personally Authorized and Encouraged&#x27; Meta&#x27;s Copyright Infringement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>扎克伯格亲自授权并推动 Meta 的版权侵权</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48026207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 477</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48026207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 429</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48026207' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report alleges Zuckerberg personally authorized and encouraged copyright infringement related to publishers, shedding light on governance of AI training data and executive-level accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份报道称扎克伯格个人授权并推动 Meta 的版权侵权，暴露出在人工智能训练数据治理与高层问责方面的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://variety.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/Meta-CEO-Mark-Zuckerberg.png?crop=0px%2C81px%2C1249px%2C703px&amp;resize=1000%2C563' data-full='https://variety.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/01/Meta-CEO-Mark-Zuckerberg.png?crop=0px%2C81px%2C1249px%2C703px&amp;resize=1000%2C563' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48109460' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48109460' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nair.sh/guides-and-opinions/communicating-your-expertise/why-senior-developers-fail-to-communicate-their-expertise' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why senior developers fail to communicate their expertise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>资深开发者为何难以传达专业能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48109460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 476</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48109460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 208</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48109460' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Senior developers often fail to translate deep expertise into accessible guidance. The piece identifies gaps in storytelling, problem framing, and sharing decision processes, not just hard skills. It offers practical tactics like narrating workflows, giving reproducible examples, and clearly outlining tradeoffs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>资深开发者常常难以将深厚的专业知识转化为易于理解的指导。文章指出在讲故事、问题表述和分享决策过程等方面存在断层，而不仅仅是技能本身的问题，并给出通过讲解工作流、提供可复现示例以及清晰陈述权衡取舍等具体做法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nair.sh/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fwsdftc%2Fwsdftc_cover.png&amp;w=3840&amp;q=75&amp;dpl=dpl_EJNiu3FBjkX8yJSwwGtmqoePQNU6' data-full='https://www.nair.sh/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fwsdftc%2Fwsdftc_cover.png&amp;w=3840&amp;q=75&amp;dpl=dpl_EJNiu3FBjkX8yJSwwGtmqoePQNU6' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48259808' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48259808' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://corrode.dev/learn/migration-guides/go-to-rust/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Migrating from Go to Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 Go 迁移到 Rust</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48259808' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 468</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48259808' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 485</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48259808' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical migration guide helps teams decide when to port Go code to Rust, with stepwise strategies, interoperability patterns, and clear trade-offs. It emphasizes balancing safety and performance gains against cost and team readiness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>迁移指南从评估时机、分步迁移模式、互操作性等角度，帮助团队将代码从 Go 迁移到 Rust。强调在性能与内存安全收益、成本以及团队准备之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://corrode.dev/learn/migration-guides/go-to-rust/social.png' data-full='https://corrode.dev/learn/migration-guides/go-to-rust/social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48238896' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48238896' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/930447/microsoft-claude-code-discontinued-notepad' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft starts canceling Claude Code licenses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软开始取消 Claude Code 的许可证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48238896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 468</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48238896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 454</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48238896' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft is canceling licenses for Claude Code, signaling a tightening stance on third-party AI tooling licensing. The move could affect developers who rely on Claude Code integrations and influence the competitive dynamics of AI coding assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软取消 Claude Code 的许可，显示对第三方 AI 工具许可的收紧态度。此举可能影响依赖 Claude Code 的开发者，并影响 AI 代码助手的竞争格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/25832912/STK095_MICROSOFT_2_CVirginia_A.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/25832912/STK095_MICROSOFT_2_CVirginia_A.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48325340' data-tags='AI,Open Source,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48325340' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://koenvangilst.nl/lab/mistral-ai-now-summit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Notes from the Mistral AI Now Summit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巴黎 Mistral AI Now Summit 现场笔记</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48325340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 461</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48325340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 205</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48325340' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paris hosted the Mistral AI Now Summit with announcements on open-weight models, partnerships, and product roadmaps. The notes capture what leaders envision for European AI tooling and governance in a rapidly evolving ecosystem. This matters for developers building open models and for investors watching AI infrastructure competition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>巴黎举行的 Mistral AI Now Summit 现场聚焦开放权重模型、伙伴关系和产品路线图。笔记记录了领导者对欧盟AI工具链与治理的展望，以及快速发展的生态带来的影响。对构建开放模型的开发者和关注AI基础设施竞争的投资者都具重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://koenvangilst.nl/og?title=Notes%20from%20the%20AI%20Now%20Summit%20by%20Mistral&amp;description=A%20few%20days%20in%20Paris%20for%20the%20Mistral%20AI%20Now%20Summit%3A%20open%20models%2C%20on-prem%20deployment%2C%20agentic%20harnesses%2C%20and%20why%20Mistral%20wants%20to%20be%20the%20European%20full-stack%20AI%20partner.&amp;type=blog' data-full='https://koenvangilst.nl/og?title=Notes%20from%20the%20AI%20Now%20Summit%20by%20Mistral&amp;description=A%20few%20days%20in%20Paris%20for%20the%20Mistral%20AI%20Now%20Summit%3A%20open%20models%2C%20on-prem%20deployment%2C%20agentic%20harnesses%2C%20and%20why%20Mistral%20wants%20to%20be%20the%20European%20full-stack%20AI%20partner.&amp;type=blog' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
